That\'s impossible in Java.\n    // Let\'s adjust: we need extra parameters to be null? Wait, count = parameters.length - 1.\n    // If parameters has only the instance, count=0.\n    // So we need methods with zero extra parameters.\n    // But we can have two overloaded methods with zero parameters? Not possible.\n    // Let\'s change approach: have one parameter extra that is null.\n    // Then count=1, tryExact=false due to null.\n    // Then getMethod will throw because types array has a null element.\n    // Then loop will find two methods that both match null with APPROXIMATE_MATCH.\n    // That\'s similar to existing test.\n    // Actually, the path requires count=0 (for loop false). Not possible.\n    // I think the path is infeasible.
